<script>
	require(['EventHandler'], function(EventHandler) {
		var pageHeaderPanel = elById('pageHeaderPanel');
		var pageHeader = elById('pageHeader');
		var offset = pageHeaderPanel.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.pageYOffset;
		offset -= parseInt(window.getComputedStyle(pageHeaderPanel).top, 10);
		
		var isSticky = false;
		function onScroll() {
			var shouldBeSticky = (window.pageYOffset > offset);
			if (shouldBeSticky !== isSticky) {
				EventHandler.fire('com.woltlab.wcf.Search', 'close');
				pageHeader.classList[shouldBeSticky ? 'add' : 'remove']('sticky');
				isSticky = shouldBeSticky;
			}
		}
		
		document.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true });
		onScroll();
	});
</script>
<script>
		require(['SoftCreatR/Bbcode/ScDereferer'], function(Dereferer) {
			(function() {
				if (typeof NodeList.prototype.forEach === "function") {
					return false;
				}
				else {
					NodeList.prototype.forEach = Array.prototype.forEach;
				}
			})();
			
			var makeURL = function(href) {
				return 'https://www.photovoltaikforum.com/core/sc-dereferer/?target=' + encodeURIComponent(href) + '&source=' + encodeURIComponent(window.location.href)			};
			
			WCF.DOMNodeInsertedHandler.addCallback('WCF.Dereferer', function() {
				Dereferer.init('["http:\/\/www.photovoltaikforum.com\/core\/","http:\/\/www.photovoltaikforum.com\/","http:\/\/www.photovoltaikforum.com\/core\/marketplace\/","http:\/\/www.photovoltaikforum.com\/wissen\/","http:\/\/www.photovoltaikforum.com\/mdb\/","http:\/\/www.photovoltaikforum.com\/core\/shop\/"]', '["*.photovoltaikforum.com","*db.photovoltaikforum.com","*commetering.de","*photovoltaik.cwalz.de","*openpetition.de","*.sonnenertrag.eu"]');

				elBySelAll('a[href^="http"]').forEach(function(link) {
					var href = elAttr(link, 'href');

					if (!elData(link, 'sc-dereferer') !== 'covered' && !Dereferer.isInternalURL(href) && !Dereferer.isWhitelisted(href)) {
						elData(link, 'sc-dereferer', 'covered');

						link.addEventListener(WCF_CLICK_EVENT, function(e) {
							elAttr(link, 'href', makeURL(href));

							// reset
							setTimeout(function() {
								elAttr(link, 'href', href);
							}, 500);
						});
					}
				});
			});
			
			WCF.DOMNodeInsertedHandler.execute();
		});
	</script>

            }
